- **ua-to-ga4**: Universal Analytics (UA) and GA4 use different names for equivalent concepts. UA was sunset on 2023-07-01; models trained before or around then guess UA field names that no longer exist in the GA4 Data API. On 2024-05-06 GA4 also renamed "conversions" to "key events" (see the conversions rows below).
